- The isolated probe boot image is 62713856 bytes, within the measured 71303168-byte BOOT limit. Its header/payload sections round-trip; no Data image is included.
- The previously uncommitted board console patch is now part of the declared series, for 61 total patches \(56 arch, 2 SoC, 2 OS, 1 board\). Isolated probe generation reuses the built source kernel and firmware, patches only a payload copy, checks the measured BOOT size limit and leaves board defaults untouched.
- The required Mu SEC entry-argument HOB integration is now versioned in package/firmware/patches and inputs.json, independently of the currently retained runtime probe.
- The normal bootimg command produced a 3993600-byte header-v2/raw-kernel/separate-DTB firmware-only check image and verified its sections and load addresses. Ramdisk is empty; no XNU kernel/payload or device boot is claimed.
- Actual bootimg invocation exposed the pinned upstream BootShim declaring only FD\_SIZE in ARM64 image\_size. The shared packer now normalizes the header with strict base/size checks; a real header-v2 raw-kernel/separate-DTB firmware-only image packs and unpacks successfully.
- The board package now contains a real T970 firmware overlay and build integration; its vendor assets stay local. Kernel board patch series remains empty.
- The shared packer now supports observed raw kernel/header-v2/separate-DTB layout, validates DTB/ramdisk addresses and byte content, and has seven passing regression cases. XNU payload inputs are still a later stage.
- A second boot-header capture confirms header v2, header\_size=1660, page\_size=4096, DTB address=0x1f00000, boot DTB bytes=1580275, and separate recovery DTBO geometry. Source boot is Magisk-modified.
- The local archive's manifest matches the intended iPadOS 17.7.11 / 21H461 target.
- The manifest's product types describe the Apple restore targets; they do not establish SM-T970 compatibility.
- The initial format-2 staging layout blocked builds; the completed device input configuration now removes the example flag without claiming a hardware boot.
- Step 2 reuses XNU 10063.141.1 / Darwin 23.6.0 and the existing profile kernelcache hash, DriverKit and TrustCache header tags. Source-level ABI scope was reviewed; private ABI and actual prelink remain unverified.
- Step 3 example-gate check invoked build kernel and bootimg against the existing configuration; both returned exit 1 with EXAMPLE device repository before building or packaging. No missing input was replaced with a fabricated path.
- tools=true only changes the generated menu; the payload/initrd image was not built because XNU kernel and Apple payload inputs are still absent, so the Tools entry and QR export remain unverified on an image.
- The full host build chain now completes at this commit: kernel.macho 51908608 bytes b3f9ab34..., payload.img 201326592 bytes a4a0bac8..., boot.img 205320192 bytes 28f3bb9f... and the 3145728-byte FD 4691d86c.... boot.json reports header v2 with ramdisk\_bytes 201326592, the captured firmware DTB hash and the built FD hash; payload.json reports the kernel, generated ADT and accepted trust cache.
- The build required a two-pass kernel flow \(bootstrap compile records, archives, final link/prelink\) and a python3 with tomllib on PATH for the archive scripts. No device write or boot was attempted; restore dumps stay in ignored storage.
- With the 56 MiB payload the rebuilt boot.img is 62713856 bytes \(payload.img 58720256, sha256 a87d42da...; boot.img sha256 d61f416a...\) and fits the 68 MiB boot partition. Odin AP tars for the test image and the Magisk restore image were generated in ignored storage; no flash has been performed.
- Added t970-skip-video.patch \(initialize\_screen guarded by t970-novc\) and t970-skip-vcattach.patch \(PE\_init\_printf TRUE path uses a null output callback under t970-novc\), and changed t970-console-bootstrap.patch to skip PE\_create\_console under t970-novc. These are diagnostic bypasses, not a replacement console.
- Ported the marble-milacy PMGR placeholder: package/ShengPlatformPower.cpp and scripts/build-platform-power.py build the 340-slot original ApplePMGR ABI bridge from the shared 21H461 kernelcache; the framework sheng-platform-power.patch is in the device series and board archives now include build-platform-power.py. The builder's audit passed unchanged-AMFI read/OR/write and original provider dispatch.
- Final console diagnostic patch set for this revision: t970-console-bootstrap calls PE\_initialize\_console under t970-novc so vinfo and the framebuffer mapping exist for the panic screen, and the t970-skip-video patch \(which skipped gc\_initialize and left VCPUTC's ticket lock uninitialized\) is removed. t970-skip-vcattach keeps PE\_putc a null writer.
- scripts/build-owner-systemos-policy.py signs and fully verifies the raw Cryptex1,SystemOS member \(806400 blocks, b4908cde\) against the same owner anchor; it is the missing SystemOS half the framework's --include-systemos bundle needs.
- Added scripts/build-verified-root-empty.py \(real VerifiedRoot crypto with a zero-length policy table, 19 KB archive, fixed at link time\) and scripts/build-ufs-stubs.py with package/UfsStubs.c. The VerifiedRoot probe archive with the 47 MiB hash table stays out of the kernel because it does not fit the 68 MiB BOOT partition.
- patches/xnu/t970-smmu-dump.patch is appended to the device series after the console and vcattach patches; it targets the end of arm\_init \(before machine\_startup\) so it stays clear of the console hunks.
- package/ShengUfs/ keeps the vendored alioth driver next to the board package; scripts/zz-build-shengufs-local.py compiles it against the kernel ABI and would replace the component archive when enabled. Both are deliberately not selected in the current archive list.
- package/ShengUfs is now the active driver copy: SmmuTakeover.h reads IDR0/1/2 at 0x0/0x4/0x8 with log2 geometry and NativeUfs.cpp adds the t970-ufs-auto-stream reuse scan; scripts/zz-build-shengufs-local.py builds the archive and must run after any component build to avoid duplicate objects.
- patches/xnu/t970-smmu-dump.patch now carries the t970-smmu-write probe \(IDR stability read plus a GFSR write-1-to-clear readback\) instead of the stream table dump; it is a guarded arm\_init diagnostic only.
- package/ShengUfs now follows the Linux-validated SMMU layout again \(IDR 0x20/0x24/0x28, NUMSMRG/NUMCB\) and carries the 128-slot auto-stream scan; the local archive remains the active build input.
- package/ShengUfs/NativeUfs.cpp adds t970-ufs-no-smmu=1, which still maps the SMMU window \(the caller dereferences it\) but skips every SMMU write; the earlier early-return variant crashed in hostBringUp and is not kept.
- The generated PHY table header and its generator live under package/firmware/Platforms/Samsung/gts7xlwifiPkg/Library/PlatformUfsLib/ with a GPL-2.0 provenance comment; docs/firmware-ufs-plan.md records the remaining work.
- package/firmware carries PlatformUfsLib \(BoardInit.c, PlatformUfsLib.inf, Sm8250UfsPhyTables.h\), DSC/FDF entries for UfsDxe, and a single merged UfsDxe source patch \(ChipInfo tolerance plus 0x9ff80004 stage words\). The firmware staging must run scripts/prepare-uefi.py from a reset checkout because same-file patches cannot overlap.
- patches/xnu/t970-smmu-dump.patch now carries the t970-ufs-status probe that maps 0x9ff80000 and panics with the firmware stage word; it is the kernel half of the firmware visibility path.
- PlatformUfsLib now links ArmSmcLib, ArmMmuLib-free DxeServicesTableLib and BaseMemoryLib; the SCM prototype lives in BoardInit.c next to the PHY replay and is selected by the same DSC/FDF entries.
- The post-survey evidence records the survey BOOT build \(c404ce26 from revision 09bff63\) and a zero-byte read log, with userdata\_written=false and recovery\_written=false for that action.

### Completion criteria

- The selected profile's inputs produce a kernel, payload and a boot image matching the SM-T970 boot path.

### Current limits

- Manifest inspection does not verify archive authenticity or integrity, driver ABI compatibility, or bootability.
- The IPSW is a local external input; its selected manifest fields are archived, but no complete image build is recorded.
- The selected profile kernelcache SHA256 f18b4df865405f2a287b84977d7aca745e056d656d9fc9df1662970ce2969de7 has not been checked against a locally extracted/decompressed kernelcache.

### Next action

- Review an exclusive bypass-context and bounded command/DMA test plan using the qualified stream fields; preserve display CB4, reject unqualified CB73 and record actual data reads separately.

### Evidence

## Diagnostics {#diagnostics}

`implemented`

### Hardware and dependencies

- Existing ADB and Magisk su can read the live FDT, physical iomem, input capabilities and primary GPT metadata while SELinux remains Enforcing.
- Android pstore lists console-ramoops-0 and pmsg-ramoops-0; their contents were not collected.
- capture.json records timestamps in Asia/Shanghai, remote commands, exit codes, boot header fields and evidence-file SHA256 values.

### Configuration

- The collector uses adb -d shell -T to preserve binary bytes and remote exit status. Evidence is an Android inventory snapshot, not an XNU verification run.
- scripts/check-platform.py resolves source pins and archive/patch plans, compiles the board DTS and checks an artificial host-only ADT against archived hardware inputs.

### Procedure

- Run python3 scripts/collect-adb-inventory.py --root --output evidence/&lt;new-capture&gt;; review per-command stderr, checksums, GPT CRC results and DT sanitization before updating this record.

### Development findings

BOOT08b90255 previously panicked on uninitialized vc\_progress\_lock via first swap console handoff. Guard now matches t970-novc bootstrap. SpringBoard sample reaches LaunchServices database context from Control Center module discovery; sustained desktop pending. See evidence/display/display-novc-v24.

- Probe control flow was checked against both the generator and the current kernel disassembly. reset-before ignores the delay and immediately issues PSCI SYSTEM\_RESET. A new after-return idle\_thread\_create probe is the next discriminating test; physical source images and restore hashes are recorded.
- Three source-patch preparation tests passed \(apply/repeat, reject changed patch, preserve unknown local edits\); an additional test used all four actual upstream SEC preimages and checked idempotence.
- The completed Step 3 archive ties actual commands, source hashes, platform checks, normal firmware compilation and boot-image round-trip to clean committed device/framework revisions.
- The strengthened dynamic-pool check caught an overlap with camera\_mem\_region that static-reservation-only checks missed. It now verifies the reduced 2 GiB window against all original pool bounds and measured-bank containment.
- The platform checker now additionally compares seven disabled controller register/IRQ descriptions, the touch/pen addresses and firmware names, dynamic reserved-pool windows and 45 original XBL regions. None is a runtime or DMA test.
- On this Magisk setup, exec-out through su inserted CR bytes into binary output; shell -T returned an FDT whose length matches its header. The collector verifies this before decoding.
- The sanitized DTS omits /chosen and identifying properties; the selected display token is captured separately. It is an input reference, not a ready-to-flash board DTB.
- The platform checker verifies CPU affinities, GIC ranges/stride, timer rate, the measured RAM-bank selection, all static reservation reg values and ADT byte order. It does not invoke ADB or produce a boot image.
- The committed platform checker produced a PASS report tied to its exact source revision and input file hashes; sanitized resolved configuration and unchanged dtc naming warnings are archived.
- Step 2 adds reproducible checks for reviewed framework/profile hashes, explicit device component pins, declared OS ABI scope and source file presence. These checks do not execute driver protocols or validate private ABI layouts.
- Archived the Step 2 PASS report with input hashes and the resolved configuration; historical platform-bootstrap evidence remains bound to its earlier source revision.
- check-platform.py now rejects a missing exporter in DSC/FDF, missing AdvancedLoggerAccessLib/QrEncoderLib/HdwPortLib/PeilessArm/DXE\_CORE mappings, a fabricated PcdAdvancedLoggerBase, or board \[payload\] tools other than true. It also ignores untracked \*.DS\_Store when verifying pinned component checkouts.
- scripts/check-build-config.py independently re-resolves the four patch series and compares them with status, re-checks every module checkout and unixnu.lock pin, and verifies the assembled tree links drivers/kits/refer to those exact sources, kits/sm-t970 to this package directory, and tree/scripts to the merged framework/SoC/device copies. Two framework behaviours are checked rather than assumed: SoC sources are deliberately not linked into tree/refer, and the feed cache may be a symlink to the same pinned checkout.
- The Step 5 checkers share scripts/fdt.py for structural FDT parsing, so the firmware blob and the board DTB are read the same way. The checker also scans the built FV when present and requires the five UniXNU/DarwinBoot modules.
- scripts/collect-storage-inputs.py derives the UFS inventory from the captured live DT and GPT, cross-checks every UFS clock/reset ID against the kona gcc/rpmh headers, and records the header hashes; it enables nothing and needs no device.
- scripts/prepare-trust-cache.py reproduces the accepted trust-cache bundle from the StaticTrustCache DER payload, runs the framework's original-system-trust builder and rejects a bundle whose SHA-256 is not in the OS profile allow-list.
- Bypassing PE\_init\_printf\(TRUE\) \(which calls vcattach\) with a null PE\_putc under t970-novc resolved the console spin: PE\_init\_iokit returned \(probe f32a04c0, \~6 s reset\) and the bootstrap thread reached bsd\_init \(probe fa16c47d, immediate reset\) on kernel d862c992. The same PE\_init\_iokit checkpoint had not reset on the earlier console-skip kernel.
- Restoring gc\_initialize fixed the invalid ticket-lock panic: with vinfo populated and the lock initialized, the panic screen renders text and the kernel reaches bsd\_init. Removing the forced panic then exposed the real next blocker: unmodified AMFI panics with 'AMFI: No PMGR?' at ConfigurationSettings.cpp:235 because no ApplePMGR provider is published. Image 041fcf8c, kernel 80cc00ad.
- With sheng-pmgr=2 and the PMGR bridge linked, unchanged AMFI passes and the kernel reaches IOKit root-device mounting: panic 'Failed to mount root device @IOKitBSDInit.cpp:984' \(kernel d4914c03, image see evidence\). This is the expected storage blocker, not a startup-chain failure.
- scripts/build-storage-bindings.py and scripts/data/native-storage-original-bindings.S are kept as the audited absolute-address binding for the original IOStorageFamily symbols; they are not referenced by any enabled archive entry.
- scripts/build-owner-systemos-policy.py is host-only qualification: it mounts the raw SystemOS member read-only, signs a manifest with the local owner anchor and verifies all 806400 blocks through the real C/RSA host dylib; no hardware or storage publication is claimed.
- A sparse DONT\_CARE segment is the working way to write a partition larger than 4 GiB through Heimdall: the bootloader expands the sparse image and skips the untouched chunks, and a 1 MiB manifest-only segment was accepted before the three full segments were attempted.
- The SMMU dump is a guarded arm\_init diagnostic \(t970-smmu-dump=1\) that maps 0x15000000 and reports the valid SMR/S2CR entries on the panic screen; it is a bring-up probe, not a UFS fix, and it leaves normal boots untouched.
- scripts/zz-build-shengufs-local.py is a host-only builder for the vendored driver; it was exercised to confirm the local archive compiles and links, and it is not part of the enabled archive list.
- The GFSR write probe \(write 0xffffffff, read back unchanged\) and the IDR0-bit30 rejection together classify the T970 SMMU as write-locked with a non-SM8550 register model; both are arm\_init/planner diagnostics, not storage fixes.
- The 128-slot auto-stream scan is a driver-side diagnostic: it reads every SMR, skips masked and non-exact entries, and only accepts a stream whose context already passes the bypass checks; it never writes the SMMU.
- The no-SMMU path keeps the SMMU IO mapping because hostBringUp dereferences it after the call; an earlier version that skipped mapPhys crashed with a null-pointer data abort \(FAR=0\), recorded here so the distinction between our patch bug and SMMU behaviour stays clear.
- scripts/gen-ufs-phy-tables.py is a host-only generator: it downloads the pinned Linux QMP UFS driver headers, resolves the QSERDES/PHY register defines and writes the C tables; it is reproducible from scratch and does not touch the device.
- The firmware bring-up uses GOP colour markers at fixed x positions plus a 0x9ff80004 stage word read back by the arm\_init probe; both are diagnostic-only and the current image still resets before XNU can report the stage word.
- The SCM probe is a board-side diagnostic in PlatformUfsLib: it issues a single fast SMC with the Qualcomm SIP owner and reports the return code through GOP markers; it does not change the SMMU or UFS state itself.
- The survey flash receipt is the device-side confirmation of the read-only UFS path; it is independent of the kernel entry probes and was captured after the full userdata rewrite.

### Completion criteria

- Every claimed UniXNU milestone has matching source/image revisions, a reproducible command and accessible firmware/kernel logs.

### Current limits

- ADB inputs and BOOT-only reset checkpoints are archived. Original BOOT restoration is pending; UART, usable UEFI log export and XNU fault capture remain unverified.

### Next action

- After committing this verified milestone, continue normal service/Data bring-up and measure memory use; preserve genuine main-marker evidence and256MiB owned-memory constraints. Current870a11da is terminal diagnostic, not a normal system image.
